Implement the following plan:

# Fix Droid E2E Tests: Three Issues

## Context

Running `E2E_AGENT=factoryai-droid go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...` produces failures in all tests due to two root causes (the third is a cascade):

1. **"Unknown tool identifier(s)"** — Tests pass Claude Code tool names (`Write`, `Bash`, `Read`, `Glob`, `Grep`) via `--enabled-tools` to `droid exec`, but Droid doesn't recognize all of them
2. **Transcripts not found on `entire/checkpoints/v1`** — Hook commands during condensation don't receive `ENTIRE_TEST_DROID_PROJECT_DIR`, so Droid's session dir can't be resolved
3. **Timeout in `TestE2E_ResumeInRelocatedRepo`** — Cascades from issue 1

## Fix 1: Drop `--enabled-tools` for Droid

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go`

The E2E tests test Entire's checkpoint/rewind behavior, not agent tool permissions. Droid has its own permission system in `.factory/settings.json`. The simplest fix is to not pass `--enabled-tools` to `droid exec` at all.

In `FactoryAIDroidRunner.RunPromptWithTools`, remove the `--enabled-tools` argument construction:

```go
// Before (lines 397-399):
if len(tools) > 0 {
    args = append(args, "--enabled-tools", strings.Join(tools, ","))
}

// After: remove this block entirely (or add a comment explaining why)
// Droid uses its own permission system in .factory/settings.json.
// E2E tests pass Claude-specific tool names that Droid doesn't recognize.
```

## Fix 2: Add `ENTIRE_TEST_DROID_PROJECT_DIR` to hook env vars

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/testenv.go`

Add `"ENTIRE_TEST_DROID_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".factory"),` after each existing `ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR` line. **6 locations:**

| Method | Hook | Line (after) |
|--------|------|-------------|
| `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` | prepare-commit-msg | 283 |
| `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` | post-commit | 324 |
| `GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks` | prepare-commit-msg | 364 |
| `GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks` | post-commit | 405 |
| `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved` | prepare-commit-msg | 437 |
| `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved` | post-commit | 495 |

## Fix 3: No separate fix needed

The `TestE2E_ResumeInRelocatedRepo` timeout is a cascade from Fix 1. Once Droid can run prompts without the unknown tools error, this test should complete within the timeout.

## Verification

```bash
# Run fmt + lint
mise run fmt && mise run lint

# Run unit + integration tests (no E2E)
mise run test:ci

# If user wants to verify E2E (costs money, only on explicit request):
# FACTORY_API_KEY=<key> E2E_AGENT=factoryai-droid go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...
```

    98→// NewClaudeCodeRunner creates a new Claude Code runner with the given config.
    99→func NewClaudeCodeRunner(config AgentRunnerConfig) *ClaudeCodeRunner {

   112→				timeout = parsed

   114→		}

   117→		}
   118→	}

   131→// IsAvailable checks if Claude CLI is installed and responds to --version.
   132→// Note: This does NOT verify authentication status. Claude Code uses OAuth
   133→// authentication (via `claude login`), not ANTHROPIC_API_KEY. If the CLI is
   134→// installed but not logged in, tests will fail at RunPrompt time.
   135→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) IsAvailable() (bool, error) {
   136→	// Check if claude CLI is in PATH
   137→	if _, err := exec.LookPath("claude"); err != nil {
   138→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not found in PATH: %w", err)
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Check if claude is working (--version doesn't require auth)
   142→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   143→	defer cancel()
   144→
   145→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", "--version")
   146→	if err := cmd.Run(); err != nil {
   147→		return false, fmt.Errorf("claude CLI not working: %w", err)
   148→	}
   149→
   150→	return true, nil
   151→}

   153→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   154→	return r.RunPromptWithTools(ctx, workDir, prompt, r.AllowedTools)
   155→}
   156→
   157→func (r *ClaudeCodeRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   158→	// Build command: claude --model <model> -p "<prompt>" --allowedTools <tools>
   159→	args := []string{
   160→		"--model", r.Model,
   161→		"-p", prompt,
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	if len(tools) > 0 {
   165→		// Claude CLI expects each tool as a separate argument after --allowedTools
   166→		// e.g., --allowedTools Edit Read Bash (not --allowedTools "Edit,Read,Bash")
   167→		args = append(args, "--allowedTools")
   168→		args = append(args, tools...)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Create context with timeout
   172→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   173→	defer cancel()
   174→
   175→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   176→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "claude", args...)
   177→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   178→
   179→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   180→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   181→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   182→
   183→	start := time.Now()
   184→	err := cmd.Run()
   185→	duration := time.Since(start)
   186→
   187→	result := &AgentResult{
   188→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   189→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   190→		Duration: duration,
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   195→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   196→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   197→		} else {
   198→			result.ExitCode = -1
   199→		}
   200→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   201→		return result, err
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	result.ExitCode = 0
   205→	return result, nil
   206→}

   267→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPrompt(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string) (*AgentResult, error) {

   270→
   271→func (r *GeminiCLIRunner) RunPromptWithTools(ctx context.Context, workDir string, prompt string, tools []string) (*AgentResult, error) {
   272→	// Build command: gemini -m <model> -p "<prompt>" --approval-mode auto_edit --allowed-tools <tools>
   273→	// --approval-mode auto_edit: auto-approves edit tools (write_file, replace) while prompting for others
   274→	// --allowed-tools: comma-separated list of tools that bypass confirmation (e.g., git commands)
   275→	args := []string{
   276→		"-m", r.Model,
   277→		"-p", prompt,
   278→		"--approval-mode", "auto_edit",
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Add default git tools that should be allowed without confirmation
   282→	defaultAllowedTools := []string{
   283→		"ShellTool(git status)",
   284→		"ShellTool(git add)",
   285→		"ShellTool(git commit)",
   286→		"ShellTool(git diff)",
   287→		"ShellTool(git log)",
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Merge with any additional tools passed in
   291→	allTools := append(defaultAllowedTools, tools...)
   292→	if len(allTools) > 0 {
   293→		args = append(args, "--allowed-tools", strings.Join(allTools, ","))
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create context with timeout
   297→	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, r.Timeout)
   298→	defer cancel()
   299→
   300→	//nolint:gosec // args are constructed from trusted config, not user input
   301→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "gemini", args...)
   302→	cmd.Dir = workDir
   303→
   304→	var stdout, stderr bytes.Buffer
   305→	cmd.Stdout = &stdout
   306→	cmd.Stderr = &stderr
   307→
   308→	start := time.Now()
   309→	err := cmd.Run()
   310→	duration := time.Since(start)
   311→
   312→	result := &AgentResult{
   313→		Stdout:   stdout.String(),
   314→		Stderr:   stderr.String(),
   315→		Duration: duration,
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if err != nil {
   319→		exitErr := &exec.ExitError{}
   320→		if errors.As(err, &exitErr) {
   321→			result.ExitCode = exitErr.ExitCode()
   322→		} else {
   323→			result.ExitCode = -1
   324→		}
   325→		//nolint:wrapcheck // error is from exec.Run, caller can check ExitCode in result
   326→		return result, err
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	result.ExitCode = 0
   330→	return result, nil
   331→}

     1→//go:build e2e
     2→
     3→package e2e
     4→
     5→import (
     6→	"context"
     7→	"crypto/sha256"
     8→	"encoding/hex"
     9→	"encoding/json"
    10→	"errors"
    11→	"os"
    12→	"os/exec"

    21→	"github.com/go-git/go-git/v5/plumbing/object"
    22→)
    23→
    24→// defaultAgent holds the agent to test with, determined in TestMain.
    25→var defaultAgent string
    26→
    27→// testBinaryPath holds the path to the CLI binary built once in TestMain.
    28→// All tests share this binary to avoid repeated builds.
    29→var testBinaryPath string
    30→
    31→// TestEnv manages an isolated test environment for E2E tests with real agent calls.
    32→type TestEnv struct {
    33→	T       *testing.T
    34→	RepoDir string
    35→	Agent   AgentRunner
    36→}
    37→
    38→// getTestBinary returns the path to the shared test binary.
    39→// It panics if TestMain hasn't run (testBinaryPath is empty).
    40→func getTestBinary() string {
    41→	if testBinaryPath == "" {
    42→		panic("testBinaryPath not set - TestMain must run before tests")
    43→	}
    44→	return testBinaryPath
    45→}
    46→
    47→// NewTestEnv creates a new isolated E2E test environment.
    48→func NewTestEnv(t *testing.T) *TestEnv {
    49→	t.Helper()
    50→
    51→	// Resolve symlinks on macOS where /var -> /private/var
    52→	repoDir := t.TempDir()
    53→	if resolved, err := filepath.EvalSymlinks(repoDir); err == nil {
    54→		repoDir = resolved
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Create agent runner
    58→	agent := NewAgentRunner(defaultAgent, AgentRunnerConfig{})
    59→
    60→	return &TestEnv{
    61→		T:       t,
    62→		RepoDir: repoDir,
    63→		Agent:   agent,
    64→	}
    65→}
    66→
    67→// NewFeatureBranchEnv creates an E2E test environment ready for testing.
    68→// It initializes the repo, creates an initial commit on main,
    69→// checks out a feature branch, and sets up agent hooks.
    70→func NewFeatureBranchEnv(t *testing.T, strategyName string) *TestEnv {
    71→	t.Helper()
    72→
    73→	env := NewTestEnv(t)
    74→	env.InitRepo()
    75→	env.WriteFile("README.md", "# Test Repository\n\nThis is a test repository for E2E testing.\n")
    76→	env.GitAdd("README.md")
    77→	env.GitCommit("Initial commit")
    78→	env.GitCheckoutNewBranch("feature/e2e-test")
    79→
    80→	// Use `entire enable` to set up everything (hooks, settings, etc.)
    81→	// This sets up .entire/settings.json and .claude/settings.json with hooks
    82→	env.RunEntireEnable(strategyName)
    83→
    84→	// Commit all files created by `entire enable` so they survive git stash -u operations.
    85→	// Without this, stash operations would stash away the hooks config and entire settings,
    86→	// causing hooks to not fire for subsequent prompts and stash pop conflicts.
    87→	// Using "git add ." is safe in test repos since we control all content.
    88→	env.GitAddAll()
    89→	env.GitCommit("Add entire and agent config")
    90→
    91→	return env
    92→}
    93→
    94→// RunEntireEnable runs `entire enable` to set up the project with hooks.
    95→// Uses the configured defaultAgent (from E2E_AGENT env var or "claude-code").
    96→func (env *TestEnv) RunEntireEnable(strategyName string) {
    97→	env.T.Helper()
    98→
    99→	args := []string{
   100→		"enable",
   101→		"--agent", defaultAgent,
   102→		"--strategy", strategyName,
   103→		"--telemetry=false",
   104→		"--force", // Force reinstall hooks in case they exist
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static
   108→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), args...)
   109→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   110→
   111→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   112→	if err != nil {
   113→		env.T.Fatalf("entire enable failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   114→	}
   115→	env.T.Logf("entire enable output: %s", output)
   116→}

   118→// InitRepo initializes a git repository in the test environment.
   119→func (env *TestEnv) InitRepo() {
   120→	env.T.Helper()
   121→
   122→	repo, err := git.PlainInit(env.RepoDir, false)
   123→	if err != nil {
   124→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to init git repo: %v", err)
   125→	}
   126→
   127→	// Configure git user for commits
   128→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
   129→	if err != nil {
   130→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get repo config: %v", err)
   131→	}
   132→	cfg.User.Name = "E2E Test User"
   133→	cfg.User.Email = "e2e-test@example.com"
   134→
   135→	// Disable GPG signing for test commits
   136→	if cfg.Raw == nil {
   137→		cfg.Raw = config.New()
   138→	}
   139→	cfg.Raw.Section("commit").SetOption("gpgsign", "false")
   140→
   141→	if err := repo.SetConfig(cfg); err != nil {
   142→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to set repo config: %v", err)
   143→	}
   144→}

   146→// WriteFile creates a file with the given content in the test repo.
   147→func (env *TestEnv) WriteFile(path, content string) {
   148→	env.T.Helper()
   149→
   150→	fullPath := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, path)
   151→
   152→	// Create parent directories
   153→	dir := filepath.Dir(fullPath)
   154→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   155→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   156→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to create directory %s: %v", dir, err)
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   160→	if err := os.WriteFile(fullPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   161→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write file %s: %v", path, err)
   162→	}
   163→}

   259→// GitCommitWithShadowHooks stages and commits files, running the prepare-commit-msg
   260→// and post-commit hooks like a real workflow.
   261→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithShadowHooks(message string, files ...string) {
   262→	env.T.Helper()
   263→
   264→	// Stage files using go-git
   265→	for _, file := range files {
   266→		env.GitAdd(file)
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   270→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   271→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   272→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   273→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   277→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   278→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile, "message")
   279→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   280→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   281→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   282→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   283→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   284→	)
   285→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   286→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	// Read the modified message
   290→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   291→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Create the commit using go-git with the modified message
   297→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   303→	if err != nil {
   304→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   305→	}
   306→
   307→	_, err = worktree.Commit(string(modifiedMsg), &git.CommitOptions{
   308→		Author: &object.Signature{
   309→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   310→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   311→			When:  time.Now(),
   312→		},
   313→	})
   314→	if err != nil {
   315→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Run post-commit hook
   319→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   320→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   321→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   322→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   323→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   324→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   325→	)
   326→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   327→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   328→	}
   329→}

   412→// GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved stages and commits files, simulating what happens when
   413→// a user removes the Entire-Checkpoint trailer during commit message editing.
   414→// This tests the opt-out behavior where removing the trailer skips condensation.
   415→func (env *TestEnv) GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved(message string, files ...string) {
   416→	env.T.Helper()
   417→
   418→	// Stage files using go-git
   419→	for _, file := range files {
   420→		env.GitAdd(file)
   421→	}
   422→
   423→	// Create a temp file for the commit message
   424→	msgFile := filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".git", "COMMIT_EDITMSG")
   425→	//nolint:gosec // test code, permissions are intentionally standard
   426→	if err := os.WriteFile(msgFile, []byte(message), 0o644); err != nil {
   427→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to write commit message file: %v", err)
   428→	}
   429→
   430→	// Run prepare-commit-msg hook
   431→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   432→	prepCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "prepare-commit-msg", msgFile)
   433→	prepCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   434→	prepCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   435→		"ENTIRE_TEST_TTY=1",
   436→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   437→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   438→	)
   439→	if output, err := prepCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   440→		env.T.Logf("prepare-commit-msg output: %s", output)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Read the modified message (with trailer added by hook)
   444→	//nolint:gosec // test code, path is from test setup
   445→	modifiedMsg, err := os.ReadFile(msgFile)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to read modified commit message: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// REMOVE the Entire-Checkpoint trailer (simulating user editing the message)
   451→	lines := strings.Split(string(modifiedMsg), "\n")
   452→	var cleanedLines []string
   453→	for _, line := range lines {
   454→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   455→			continue
   456→		}
   457→		if strings.Contains(line, "Remove the Entire-Checkpoint trailer") {
   458→			continue
   459→		}
   460→		if strings.Contains(line, "trailer will be added to your next commit") {
   461→			continue
   462→		}
   463→		cleanedLines = append(cleanedLines, line)
   464→	}
   465→	cleanedMsg := strings.TrimRight(strings.Join(cleanedLines, "\n"), "\n") + "\n"
   466→
   467→	// Create the commit using go-git with the cleaned message (no trailer)
   468→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   469→	if err != nil {
   470→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   474→	if err != nil {
   475→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get worktree: %v", err)
   476→	}
   477→
   478→	_, err = worktree.Commit(cleanedMsg, &git.CommitOptions{
   479→		Author: &object.Signature{
   480→			Name:  "E2E Test User",
   481→			Email: "e2e-test@example.com",
   482→			When:  time.Now(),
   483→		},
   484→	})
   485→	if err != nil {
   486→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to commit: %v", err)
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Run post-commit hook - since trailer was removed, no condensation should happen
   490→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are from trusted test setup, no context needed
   491→	postCmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "hooks", "git", "post-commit")
   492→	postCmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   493→	postCmd.Env = append(os.Environ(),
   494→		"ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".claude"),
   495→		"ENTIRE_TEST_GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR="+filepath.Join(env.RepoDir, ".gemini"),
   496→	)
   497→	if output, err := postCmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
   498→		env.T.Logf("post-commit output: %s", output)
   499→	}
   500→}

   530→
   531→// RewindPoint mirrors the rewind --list JSON output.
   532→type RewindPoint struct {
   533→	ID               string    `json:"id"`
   534→	Message          string    `json:"message"`
   535→	MetadataDir      string    `json:"metadata_dir"`
   536→	Date             time.Time `json:"date"`
   537→	IsTaskCheckpoint bool      `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   538→	ToolUseID        string    `json:"tool_use_id"`
   539→	IsLogsOnly       bool      `json:"is_logs_only"`
   540→	CondensationID   string    `json:"condensation_id"`
   541→}
   542→
   543→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points using the CLI.
   544→func (env *TestEnv) GetRewindPoints() []RewindPoint {
   545→	env.T.Helper()
   546→
   547→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, args are static, no context needed
   548→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--list")
   549→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   550→
   551→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   552→	if err != nil {
   553→		env.T.Fatalf("rewind --list failed: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   554→	}
   555→
   556→	// Parse JSON output
   557→	var jsonPoints []struct {
   558→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   559→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   560→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   561→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   562→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   563→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   564→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   565→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id"`
   566→	}
   567→
   568→	if err := json.Unmarshal(output, &jsonPoints); err != nil {
   569→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to parse rewind points: %v\nOutput: %s", err, output)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	points := make([]RewindPoint, len(jsonPoints))
   573→	for i, jp := range jsonPoints {
   574→		//nolint:errcheck // date parsing failure is acceptable, defaults to zero time
   575→		date, _ := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, jp.Date)
   576→		points[i] = RewindPoint{
   577→			ID:               jp.ID,
   578→			Message:          jp.Message,
   579→			MetadataDir:      jp.MetadataDir,
   580→			Date:             date,
   581→			IsTaskCheckpoint: jp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   582→			ToolUseID:        jp.ToolUseID,
   583→			IsLogsOnly:       jp.IsLogsOnly,
   584→			CondensationID:   jp.CondensationID,
   585→		}
   586→	}
   587→
   588→	return points
   589→}
   590→
   591→// Rewind performs a rewind to the specified commit ID using the CLI.
   592→func (env *TestEnv) Rewind(commitID string) error {
   593→	env.T.Helper()
   594→
   595→	//nolint:gosec,noctx // test code, commitID is from test setup, no context needed
   596→	cmd := exec.Command(getTestBinary(), "rewind", "--to", commitID)
   597→	cmd.Dir = env.RepoDir
   598→
   599→	output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput()
   600→	if err != nil {
   601→		return errors.New("rewind failed: " + string(output))
   602→	}
   603→
   604→	env.T.Logf("Rewind output: %s", output)
   605→	return nil
   606→}

   679→// GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory walks backwards from HEAD and returns
   680→// the checkpoint ID from the first commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   681→// Returns an error if no checkpoint trailer is found in any commit.
   682→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointIDFromHistory() (string, error) {
   683→	env.T.Helper()
   684→
   685→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   686→	if err != nil {
   687→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	head, err := repo.Head()
   691→	if err != nil {
   692→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to get HEAD: %v", err)
   693→	}
   694→
   695→	commitIter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: head.Hash()})
   696→	if err != nil {
   697→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to iterate commits: %v", err)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	var checkpointID string
   701→	//nolint:errcheck,gosec // ForEach callback returns error to stop iteration, not a real error
   702→	commitIter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   703→		// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   704→		for _, line := range strings.Split(c.Message, "\n") {
   705→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   706→			if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   707→				checkpointID = strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   708→				return errors.New("stop iteration")
   709→			}
   710→		}
   711→		return nil
   712→	})
   713→
   714→	if checkpointID == "" {
   715→		return "", errors.New("no commit with Entire-Checkpoint trailer found in history")
   716→	}
   717→
   718→	return checkpointID, nil
   719→}

   721→// GetLatestCheckpointID returns the checkpoint ID from HEAD commit, or empty string if none.
   722→// This is a convenience wrapper that doesn't fail the test on missing checkpoint.
   723→func (env *TestEnv) GetLatestCheckpointID() string {
   724→	env.T.Helper()
   725→
   726→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   727→	if err != nil {
   728→		return ""
   729→	}
   730→
   731→	head, err := repo.Head()
   732→	if err != nil {
   733→		return ""
   734→	}
   735→
   736→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   737→	if err != nil {
   738→		return ""
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	// Look for Entire-Checkpoint trailer in HEAD commit only
   742→	for _, line := range strings.Split(commit.Message, "\n") {
   743→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   744→		if strings.HasPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:") {
   745→			return strings.TrimSpace(strings.TrimPrefix(line, "Entire-Checkpoint:"))
   746→		}
   747→	}
   748→	return ""
   749→}

   751→// safeIDPrefix returns first 12 chars of ID or the full ID if shorter.

   757→	return id

   927→
   928→// ReadFileFromBranch reads a file's content from a specific branch's tree.
   929→// Returns the content and true if found, empty string and false if not found.
   930→func (env *TestEnv) ReadFileFromBranch(branchName, filePath string) (string, bool) {
   931→	env.T.Helper()
   932→
   933→	repo, err := git.PlainOpen(env.RepoDir)
   934→	if err != nil {
   935→		env.T.Fatalf("failed to open git repo: %v", err)
   936→	}
   937→
   938→	// Get the branch reference
   939→	ref, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(branchName), true)
   940→	if err != nil {
   941→		// Try as a remote-style ref
   942→		ref, err = repo.Reference(plumbing.ReferenceName("refs/heads/"+branchName), true)
   943→		if err != nil {
   944→			return "", false
   945→		}
   946→	}
   947→
   948→	// Get the commit
   949→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   950→	if err != nil {
   951→		return "", false
   952→	}
   953→
   954→	// Get the tree
   955→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   956→	if err != nil {
   957→		return "", false
   958→	}
   959→
   960→	// Get the file
   961→	file, err := tree.File(filePath)
   962→	if err != nil {
   963→		return "", false
   964→	}
   965→
   966→	// Get the content
   967→	content, err := file.Contents()
   968→	if err != nil {
   969→		return "", false
   970→	}
   971→
   972→	return content, true
   973→}

   975→// CheckpointValidation contains expected values for checkpoint validation.
   976→type CheckpointValidation struct {
   977→	// CheckpointID is the expected checkpoint ID
   978→	CheckpointID string
   979→
   980→	// Strategy is the expected strategy name (optional)
   981→	Strategy string
   982→
   983→	// FilesTouched are the expected files in files_touched (optional)
   984→	FilesTouched []string
   985→
   986→	// ExpectedPrompts are strings that should appear in prompt.txt (optional)
   987→	ExpectedPrompts []string
   988→
   989→	// ExpectedTranscriptContent are strings that should appear in full.jsonl (optional)
   990→	ExpectedTranscriptContent []string
   991→}
   992→
   993→// ValidateCheckpoint performs comprehensive validation of a checkpoint on the metadata branch.
   994→func (env *TestEnv) ValidateCheckpoint(v CheckpointValidation) {
   995→	env.T.Helper()
   996→
   997→	metadataBranch := "entire/checkpoints/v1"
   998→
   999→	// Compute sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
  1000→	if len(v.CheckpointID) < 3 {
  1001→		env.T.Fatalf("Checkpoint ID too short: %s", v.CheckpointID)
  1002→	}
  1003→	shardedPath := v.CheckpointID[:2] + "/" + v.CheckpointID[2:]
  1004→
  1005→	// Validate root metadata.json exists and has expected fields
  1006→	summaryPath := shardedPath + "/metadata.json"
  1007→	summaryContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, summaryPath)
  1008→	if !found {
  1009→		env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary not found at %s", summaryPath)
  1010→	} else {
  1011→		var summary map[string]any
  1012→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary); err != nil {
  1013→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse CheckpointSummary: %v", err)
  1014→		} else {
  1015→			// Validate checkpoint_id
  1016→			if cpID, ok := summary["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1017→				env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", summary["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1018→			}
  1019→			// Validate strategy if specified
  1020→			if v.Strategy != "" {
  1021→				if strategy, ok := summary["strategy"].(string); !ok || strategy != v.Strategy {
  1022→					env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.strategy = %v, want %s", summary["strategy"], v.Strategy)
  1023→				}
  1024→			}
  1025→			// Validate sessions array exists
  1026→			if sessions, ok := summary["sessions"].([]any); !ok || len(sessions) == 0 {
  1027→				env.T.Error("CheckpointSummary.sessions should have at least one entry")
  1028→			}
  1029→			// Validate files_touched if specified
  1030→			if len(v.FilesTouched) > 0 {
  1031→				if filesTouched, ok := summary["files_touched"].([]any); ok {
  1032→					touchedSet := make(map[string]bool)
  1033→					for _, f := range filesTouched {
  1034→						if s, ok := f.(string); ok {
  1035→							touchedSet[s] = true
  1036→						}
  1037→					}
  1038→					for _, expected := range v.FilesTouched {
  1039→						if !touchedSet[expected] {
  1040→							env.T.Errorf("CheckpointSummary.files_touched missing %q", expected)
  1041→						}
  1042→					}
  1043→				}
  1044→			}
  1045→		}
  1046→	}
  1047→
  1048→	// Validate session metadata.json exists
  1049→	sessionMetadataPath := shardedPath + "/0/metadata.json"
  1050→	sessionContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, sessionMetadataPath)
  1051→	if !found {
  1052→		env.T.Errorf("Session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1053→	} else {
  1054→		var metadata map[string]any
  1055→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &metadata); err != nil {
  1056→			env.T.Errorf("Failed to parse session metadata: %v", err)
  1057→		} else {
  1058→			// Validate checkpoint_id matches
  1059→			if cpID, ok := metadata["checkpoint_id"].(string); !ok || cpID != v.CheckpointID {
  1060→				env.T.Errorf("Session metadata.checkpoint_id = %v, want %s", metadata["checkpoint_id"], v.CheckpointID)
  1061→			}
  1062→			// Validate created_at exists
  1063→			if _, ok := metadata["created_at"].(string); !ok {
  1064→				env.T.Error("Session metadata.created_at should exist")
  1065→			}
  1066→		}
  1067→	}
  1068→
  1069→	// Validate transcript is valid JSONL (only enforce for Claude Code, log warnings for others)
  1070→	transcriptPath := shardedPath + "/0/full.jsonl"
  1071→	transcriptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, transcriptPath)
  1072→	if !found {
  1073→		env.T.Errorf("Transcript not found at %s", transcriptPath)
  1074→	} else {
  1075→		// Check each line is valid JSON
  1076→		// Only enforce JSONL format for Claude Code - other agents may have different formats
  1077→		isClaudeCode := env.Agent.Name() == AgentNameClaudeCode
  1078→		lines := strings.Split(transcriptContent, "\n")
  1079→		validLines := 0
  1080→		for i, line := range lines {
  1081→			line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
  1082→			if line == "" {
  1083→				continue
  1084→			}
  1085→			validLines++
  1086→			var obj map[string]any
  1087→			if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &obj); err != nil {
  1088→				if isClaudeCode {
  1089→					env.T.Errorf("Transcript line %d is not valid JSON: %v", i+1, err)
  1090→				}
  1091→				// For non-Claude agents, just log (transcript format may differ)
  1092→			}
  1093→		}
  1094→		if validLines == 0 {
  1095→			env.T.Error("Transcript is empty (no valid JSONL lines)")
  1096→		}
  1097→		// Check expected content
  1098→		for _, expected := range v.ExpectedTranscriptContent {
  1099→			if !strings.Contains(transcriptContent, expected) {
  1100→				env.T.Errorf("Transcript should contain %q", expected)
  1101→			}
  1102→		}
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	// Validate prompt.txt contains expected prompts
  1106→	if len(v.ExpectedPrompts) > 0 {
  1107→		promptPath := shardedPath + "/0/prompt.txt"
  1108→		promptContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, promptPath)
  1109→		if !found {
  1110→			env.T.Errorf("Prompt file not found at %s", promptPath)
  1111→		} else {
  1112→			for _, expected := range v.ExpectedPrompts {
  1113→				if !strings.Contains(promptContent, expected) {
  1114→					env.T.Errorf("Prompt file should contain %q", expected)
  1115→				}
  1116→			}
  1117→		}
  1118→	}
  1119→
  1120→	// Validate content hash exists and matches transcript
  1121→	hashPath := shardedPath + "/0/content_hash.txt"
  1122→	hashContent, found := env.ReadFileFromBranch(metadataBranch, hashPath)
  1123→	if !found {
  1124→		env.T.Errorf("Content hash not found at %s", hashPath)
  1125→	} else if transcriptContent != "" {
  1126→		// Verify hash matches
  1127→		hash := sha256.Sum256([]byte(transcriptContent))
  1128→		expectedHash := "sha256:" + hex.EncodeToString(hash[:])
  1129→		storedHash := strings.TrimSpace(hashContent)
  1130→		if storedHash != expectedHash {
  1131→			env.T.Errorf("Content hash mismatch:\n  stored:   %s\n  expected: %s", storedHash, expectedHash)
  1132→		}
  1133→	}
  1134→}
  1135→

> AGENT

All clean — fmt, lint, and tests pass.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Why these two fixes work together:** The E2E test infrastructure simulates the full hook lifecycle (prepare-commit-msg → commit → post-commit) for testing checkpoint condensation. During condensation, the hooks invoke `entire hooks git post-commit`, which needs to resolve the agent's session directory to find transcripts. Each agent stores sessions in a different project directory (`.claude/`, `.gemini/`, `.factory/`), and the `ENTIRE_TEST_*_PROJECT_DIR` env vars tell the hooks where to look in the test environment (since there's no real agent process running). Without the Droid env var, the hooks couldn't find Droid's transcripts, causing condensation to fail silently — the checkpoint would be created without transcript data on `entire/checkpoints/v1`.

**Leaky abstraction lesson:** The `--enabled-tools` flag was a Claude Code-specific concept leaking through the `AgentRunner` interface into a Droid runner. Each agent has its own permission model — Droid uses `.factory/settings.json`, Claude uses `--allowedTools`, Gemini uses `--allowed-tools` with different tool name formats. The E2E tests only need to verify Entire's behavior (checkpoints, rewind), not agent tool permissions.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

**Summary of changes:**

1. **`agent_runner.go`** — Removed the `--enabled-tools` argument from `FactoryAIDroidRunner.RunPromptWithTools`. Droid has its own permission system and doesn't recognize Claude Code tool names like `Write`, `Bash`, etc.

2. **`testenv.go`** — Added `ENTIRE_TEST_DROID_PROJECT_DIR` pointing to `.factory/` in all 6 hook invocation sites (`GitCommitWithShadowHooks`, `GitCommitStagedWithShadowHooks`, `GitCommitWithTrailerRemoved` — each has prepare-commit-msg and post-commit). This allows the hooks to resolve Droid's session directory during condensation.

     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→Running `E2E_AGENT=factoryai-droid go test -tags=e2e ./cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/...` produces failures in all tests due to two root causes (the third is a cascade):
     6→

    12→
    13→**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/e2e_test/agent_runner.go`
    14→
    15→The E2E tests test Entire's checkpoint/rewind behavior, not agent tool permissions. Droid has its own permission system in `.factory/settings.json`. The simplest fix is to not pass `--enabled-tools` to `droid exec` at all.
    16→
    17→In `FactoryAIDroidRunner.RunPromptWithTools`, remove the `--enabled-tools` argument construction:

    25→// After: remove this block entirely (or add a comment explaining why)

## Summary

The **"not implemented" error** for `entire resume feature/e2e-test --force` with Factory AI Droid comes from a direct stub in the Droid agent implementation. Here's the exact code path:

### Error Source

**File:** `/Users/alisha/Projects/wt/factoryai-agent/cmd/entire/cli/agent/factoryaidroid/factoryaidroid.go`, lines 136-139

```go
// WriteSession is not implemented for Factory AI Droid.
func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) WriteSession(_ *agent.AgentSession) error {
	return errors.New("not implemented")
}
```

### Complete Call Chain

1. **Resume Command** (`cmd/entire/cli/resume.go:551-557`) calls `ag.WriteSession(agentSession)`:
   ```go
   // Create an AgentSession with the native data
   agentSession := &agent.AgentSession{
       SessionID:  sessionID,
       AgentName:  ag.Name(),
       RepoPath:   repoRoot,
       SessionRef: sessionLogPath,
       NativeData: logContent,
       ExportData: exportData,
   }
   
   // Write the session using the agent's WriteSession method
   if err := ag.WriteSession(agentSession); err != nil {
       logging.Error(ctx, "resume session failed during write",
           slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
           slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
           slog.String("error", err.Error()),
       )
       return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session: %w", err)
   }
   ```

2. **Agent Interface** (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/agent.go:84-85`) defines `WriteSession` as a required method:
   ```go
   // WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
   WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
   ```

3. **Factory AI Droid Implementation** (`factoryaidroid.go:137-138`) returns the stub:
   ```go
   return errors.New("not implemented")
   ```

4. **Log Output** shows the exact location:
   ```
   ERROR resume session failed during write component=resume agent=factoryai-droid checkpoint_id=54959f6b2112 session_id=63c452fc-876b-442a-9484-f5405a72985f error="not implemented"
   ```

### Related Stub Method

Factory AI Droid also has a companion stub method `ReadSession` (lines 131-134) that's similarly not implemented:

```go
// ReadSession is not implemented for Factory AI Droid.
func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ReadSession(_ *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
	return nil, errors.New("not implemented")
}
```

### What Needs to Be Implemented

For resume to work with Factory AI Droid, the `WriteSession` method needs to:
1. Take the `AgentSession` struct containing the session ID, repo path, and native transcript data
2. Write the session transcript to the appropriate location (Factory AI Droid stores sessions in `~/.factory/sessions/<sanitized-repo-path>/`)
3. Use the `ResolveSessionFile` helper (line 127-129) to determine the exact file path: `filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")`

The session data should be written using the `NativeData` field from the `AgentSession` struct, which contains the JSONL transcript bytes restored from the checkpoint.

Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive analysis. Let me compile my findings:

## Root Cause Analysis: Multi-Commit E2E Test Transcript Issue

Based on my analysis of the codebase, I've identified the root cause of the missing transcripts in the second commit:

### **The Core Issue**

When a second commit happens during the same session in a multi-commit scenario, the session's `TranscriptPath` field is **stale** at the time of condensation. Here's the critical flow:

#### **Session Path Initialization**
At `InitializeSession` (first prompt), the code sets:
```go
state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath  // e.g., "~/.factory/sessions/repo-name/session-id.jsonl"
```
This path is stored in the session state file and becomes stale because it's an absolute path captured at session initialization time.

#### **The Problem During Second Commit**

When the second `GitCommitWithShadowHooks` is called:

1. **PrepareCommitMsg Hook** adds a checkpoint trailer with a new checkpoint ID
2. **PostCommit Hook** fires and loads the session state from disk
3. The condensation flow calls `CondenseSession()` which:
   ```go
   sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, 
                                           state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath,  // Uses stale path
                                           state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   ```

4. Inside `extractSessionData()` at line 398-402:
   ```go
   if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
       if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 {
           fullTranscript = string(liveData)
       }
   }
   ```

**The problem:** The `state.TranscriptPath` is a relative or absolute path to the Droid transcript that was resolved ONCE during `InitializeSession`. But between commits:
- The session state file persists with the same `TranscriptPath`
- The `TranscriptPath` is never updated to reflect any dynamic changes
- For Droid specifically, the session directory is computed as `~/.factory/sessions/<sanitized-repo-path>/` (from `GetSessionDir()` line 114-124 in `factoryaidroid.go`)

#### **Why It Works for First Commit, Not Second**

- **First commit**: The `TranscriptPath` was just set in `InitializeSession`, so it's recent/accurate
- **Second commit**: The `TranscriptPath` from session state may be stale because:
  1. It's not re-resolved before each commit
  2. For Droid, the session directory path could have changed if repo location changed (though unlikely in E2E tests)
  3. More importantly: After `condenseAndUpdateState()` is called for the first commit, the session state is saved with `CheckpointTranscriptStart` updated to the total transcript lines (line 830 in manual_commit_hooks.go), but `TranscriptPath` is not re-resolved

#### **The Root Cause in Context**

Looking at `manual_commit_session.go` line 1433-1434:
```go
if transcriptPath != "" && state.TranscriptPath != transcriptPath {
    state.TranscriptPath = transcriptPath
}
```

This update happens only during `InitializeSession` when a new `transcriptPath` parameter is passed. But after the first condensation, when the second commit's post-commit hook executes:

1. No `transcriptPath` is passed (it comes from the Hook Input which may be outdated)
2. The session state's `TranscriptPath` is not re-resolved from `resolveTranscriptPath()` 
3. Therefore, when condensation tries to read the live transcript, it uses the stale path

### **Solution Approach**

The fix requires **re-resolving the TranscriptPath dynamically** before each condensation in the PostCommit hook, rather than relying on the cached value in session state.

Specifically, in `PostCommit()` before calling `CondenseSession()`, the code should:
1. Load the session state from disk
2. Re-resolve the `TranscriptPath` using `resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)` 
3. Update the session state's `TranscriptPath` if it differs
4. Use the fresh path for condensation

Or more directly, `CondenseSession()` could accept an optional parameter to re-compute the transcript path dynamically, or the caller should ensure the transcript path is current before calling it.

This aligns with the existing design principle noted in `manual_commit_condensation.go` line 363:
> "transcript paths are always computed dynamically from the current repo location (via `agent.GetSessionDir` + `agent.ResolveSessionFile`), never stored in checkpoint metadata. This ensures restore/rewind works after repo relocation or across machines."

However, the **session state itself is storing the transcript path**, which becomes stale between commits.

Based on my comprehensive analysis, here's the debug report:

## Debug Report: TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingSimulated Failure

### Problem Summary
The test fails with "0 is not >= 1" when checking for rewind points after the agent completes. The test expects:
1. Agent runs and modifies `partial.go`
2. Stop hook fires → creates a checkpoint on shadow branch
3. Rewind points should be available before user commits

But currently: **0 rewind points exist**

### Root Cause
The issue is in the **hook invocation flow for Factory AI Droid**:

1. **Hook Registration** ✓ Works
   - `RunEntireEnable()` installs hooks in `.factory/settings.json`
   - Hooks are registered correctly (stop hook → `entire hooks factoryai-droid stop`)

2. **Hook Execution** ✗ Likely Issue
   - The test runs `droid exec --cwd <dir> --auto medium ...` (agent_runner.go:390-404)
   - **Droid MUST invoke registered hooks after execution completes**
   - If Droid doesn't call the stop hook, the entire lifecycle chain breaks

### The Checkpoint Creation Chain

```
Droid execution completes
  ↓
Stop hook invoked by Droid (fire: entire hooks factoryai-droid stop)
  ↓
Hook reads session_id + transcript_path from stdin
  ↓
ParseHookEvent(HookNameStop, stdin) → Returns TurnEnd event
  ↓
DispatchLifecycleEvent → handleLifecycleTurnEnd()
  ↓
strat.SaveStep(ctx) → WriteTemporary() → Creates checkpoint on shadow branch
  ↓
GetRewindPoints() finds checkpoint via `rewind --list` CLI command
```

**If the stop hook isn't called: the entire chain stops, and no checkpoint is created.**

### Critical Dependencies

For checkpoints to appear as rewind points:

1. **Droid must invoke the stop hook** - Not controlled by Entire; depends on Droid's implementation
2. **Stop hook must receive valid input** - Droid must pass JSON with:
   - `session_id` (string)
   - `transcript_path` (string, absolute path to transcript file)
3. **Transcript file must exist** - lifecycle.go:171-173 checks this
4. **SaveStep must not skip** - If no changes detected, checkpoint is skipped (manual_commit_git.go:104-114)

### Recommendations for Debugging

1. **Check if Droid hooks are actually firing**:
   - Add logging to the hook commands to verify they're being invoked
   - Check `.entire/logs/` for any hook execution logs

2. **Verify Droid integration with hooks**:
   - Check if `droid exec` actually calls registered hooks from `.factory/settings.json`
   - May need to verify Droid's documentation on hook support

3. **Test the hook infrastructure directly**:
   - Manually call `entire hooks factoryai-droid stop` with valid JSON input
   - Verify checkpoint is created

4. **Check session directory creation**:
   - lifecycle.go:187 creates `.entire/metadata/<session-id>/`
   - If this fails, SaveStep may not be called

5. **Review Droid's hook mechanism**:
   - The test assumes Droid invokes hooks like Claude Code does
   - But Droid's hook mechanism may be different (asynchronous, event-driven, etc.)

    27→// ParseHookEvent translates a Factory AI Droid hook into a normalized lifecycle Event.
    28→// Returns nil if the hook has no lifecycle significance.
    29→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ParseHookEvent(hookName string, stdin io.Reader) (*agent.Event, error) {
    30→	switch hookName {
    31→	case HookNameSessionStart:
    32→		return f.parseSessionStart(stdin)
    33→	case HookNameUserPromptSubmit:
    34→		return f.parseTurnStart(stdin)
    35→	case HookNameStop:
    36→		return f.parseTurnEnd(stdin)
    37→	case HookNameSessionEnd:
    38→		return f.parseSessionEnd(stdin)
    39→	case HookNamePreToolUse:
    40→		return f.parseSubagentStart(stdin)
    41→	case HookNamePostToolUse:
    42→		return f.parseSubagentEnd(stdin)
    43→	case HookNamePreCompact:
    44→		return f.parseCompaction(stdin)
    45→	case HookNameSubagentStop, HookNameNotification:
    46→		// Acknowledged hooks with no lifecycle action
    47→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil event = no lifecycle action
    48→	default:
    49→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // Unknown hooks have no lifecycle action
    50→	}
    51→}

    64→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line offset.

    66→	lines, currentPos, err := ParseDroidTranscript(path, startOffset)
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    69→	}
    70→	files := ExtractModifiedFiles(lines)
    71→	return files, currentPos, nil

    74→// ExtractPrompts extracts user prompts from the transcript starting at the given line offset.
    75→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractPrompts(sessionRef string, fromOffset int) ([]string, error) {
    76→	lines, _, err := ParseDroidTranscript(sessionRef, fromOffset)
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	var prompts []string
    82→	for i := range lines {
    83→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeUser {
    84→			continue
    85→		}
    86→		content := transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[i].Message)
    87→		if content != "" {
    88→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content))
    89→		}
    90→	}
    91→	return prompts, nil
    92→}

    94→// ExtractSummary extracts the last assistant message as a session summary.
    95→func (f *FactoryAIDroidAgent) ExtractSummary(sessionRef string) (string, error) {
    96→	data, err := os.ReadFile(sessionRef) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from agent hook input
    97→	if err != nil {
    98→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    99→	}
   100→	lines, err := ParseDroidTranscriptFromBytes(data)
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   106→		if lines[i].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant {
   107→			continue
   108→		}
   109→		var msg transcript.AssistantMessage
   110→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   111→			continue
   112→		}
   113→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   114→			if block.Type == transcript.ContentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   115→				return block.Text, nil
   116→			}
   117→		}
   118→	}
   119→	return "", nil
   120→}

   420→	}

   423→// TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingWithGitAddP tests partial staging with git add -p.
   424→//
   425→// Scenario 7: Partial Staging with `git add -p`
   426→// - Agent makes multiple changes to a single file
   427→// - User stages only part of the changes (simulated with partial write)
   428→// - Content-aware carry-forward detects partial commit
   429→// - Remaining changes carried forward to next commit
   430→func TestE2E_Scenario7_PartialStagingSimulated(t *testing.T) {
   431→	t.Parallel()
   432→
   433→	env := NewFeatureBranchEnv(t, "manual-commit")
   434→
   435→	// Create partial.go as an existing tracked file first.
   436→	// For MODIFIED files (vs NEW files), content-aware detection always
   437→	// creates checkpoints regardless of content changes. This allows us
   438→	// to test the partial staging scenario.
   439→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", "package main\n\n// placeholder\n")
   440→	env.GitAdd("partial.go")
   441→	env.GitCommit("Add placeholder partial.go")
   442→
   443→	// Agent modifies the file with multiple functions
   444→	t.Log("Agent modifying file with multiple functions")
   445→	multiLinePrompt := `Replace the contents of partial.go with this exact content:
   446→package main
   447→
   448→func First() int {
   449→	return 1
   450→}
   451→
   452→func Second() int {
   453→	return 2
   454→}
   455→
   456→func Third() int {
   457→	return 3
   458→}
   459→
   460→func Fourth() int {
   461→	return 4
   462→}
   463→
   464→Replace the file with exactly this content, nothing else.`
   465→
   466→	result, err := env.RunAgent(multiLinePrompt)
   467→	require.NoError(t, err)
   468→	AssertAgentSuccess(t, result, err)
   469→
   470→	require.True(t, env.FileExists("partial.go"))
   471→
   472→	// Check rewind points before commit
   473→	pointsBefore := env.GetRewindPoints()
   474→	t.Logf("Rewind points before commit: %d", len(pointsBefore))
   475→
   476→	// Simulate partial staging by temporarily replacing file with partial content
   477→	// Save the full content
   478→	fullContent := env.ReadFile("partial.go")
   479→	t.Logf("Full content length: %d bytes", len(fullContent))
   480→
   481→	// Write partial content (only first two functions)
   482→	partialContent := `package main
   483→
   484→func First() int {
   485→	return 1
   486→}
   487→
   488→func Second() int {
   489→	return 2
   490→}
   491→`
   492→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", partialContent)
   493→
   494→	// Commit the partial content
   495→	t.Log("Committing partial content (First and Second functions)")
   496→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add first two functions", "partial.go")
   497→
   498→	// Verify first checkpoint was created
   499→	checkpointIDs := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   500→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDs), 1, "Should have first checkpoint")
   501→	t.Logf("First checkpoint ID: %s", checkpointIDs[0])
   502→
   503→	// Restore the full content (simulating remaining changes still in worktree)
   504→	env.WriteFile("partial.go", fullContent)
   505→
   506→	// Commit the remaining content
   507→	t.Log("Committing full content (all functions)")
   508→	env.GitCommitWithShadowHooks("Add remaining functions", "partial.go")
   509→
   510→	// Verify second checkpoint was created
   511→	checkpointIDsAfter := env.GetAllCheckpointIDsFromHistory()
   512→	require.GreaterOrEqual(t, len(checkpointIDsAfter), 2, "Should have two checkpoints")
   513→	t.Logf("Checkpoint IDs: %v", checkpointIDsAfter)
   514→
   515→	// Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID
   516→	assert.NotEqual(t, checkpointIDsAfter[0], checkpointIDsAfter[1],
   517→		"Each commit should have its own unique checkpoint ID")
   518→
   519→	// Validate checkpoints have proper metadata and transcripts
