    38→
    39→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    40→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    41→//
    42→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    43→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    44→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    45→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    46→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    47→
    48→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    49→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    50→		return errors.New("invalid checkpoint options: checkpoint ID is required")
    51→	}
    52→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    53→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    54→	}
    55→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    56→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    57→	}
    58→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    59→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
    63→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
    64→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
    65→	}
    66→
    67→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
    68→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Use sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    74→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
    75→
    76→	// Track task metadata path for commit trailer
    77→	var taskMetadataPath string
    78→
    79→	// Handle task checkpoints
    80→	if opts.IsTask && opts.ToolUseID != "" {
    81→		taskMetadataPath, err = s.writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries)
    82→		if err != nil {
    83→			return err
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Write standard checkpoint entries (transcript, prompts, context, metadata)
    88→	if err := s.writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries); err != nil {
    89→		return err
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Build and commit
    93→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
    94→	if err != nil {
    95→		return err
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	commitMsg := s.buildCommitMessage(opts, taskMetadataPath)
    99→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
   100→	if err != nil {
   101→		return err
   102→	}
   103→
   104→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   105→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
   106→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// getSessionsBranchEntries returns the sessions branch reference and flattened tree entries.
   114→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {
   115→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   116→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   117→	if err != nil {
   118→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get sessions branch reference: %w", err)
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	parentCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   122→	if err != nil {
   123→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   132→	if err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   133→		return nil, nil, err
   134→	}
   135→
   136→	return ref, entries, nil
   137→}
   138→
   139→// writeTaskCheckpointEntries writes task-specific checkpoint entries and returns the task metadata path.
   140→func (s *GitStore) writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   141→	taskPath := basePath + "tasks/" + opts.ToolUseID + "/"
   142→
   143→	if opts.IsIncremental {
   144→		return s.writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   145→	}
   146→	return s.writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   147→}
   148→
   149→// writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint writes an incremental checkpoint file during task execution.
   150→func (s *GitStore) writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   151→	incData, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.IncrementalData)
   152→	if err != nil {
   153→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to redact incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   154→	}
   155→	checkpoint := incrementalCheckpointData{
   156→		Type:      opts.IncrementalType,
   157→		ToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,
   158→		Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
   159→		Data:      incData,
   160→	}
   161→	cpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   162→	if err != nil {
   163→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   164→	}
   165→	cpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   168→	}
   169→
   170→	cpFilename := fmt.Sprintf("%03d-%s.json", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)
   171→	cpPath := taskPath + "checkpoints/" + cpFilename
   172→	entries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   173→		Name: cpPath,
   174→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   175→		Hash: cpBlobHash,
   176→	}
   177→	return cpPath, nil
   178→}
   179→
   180→// writeFinalTaskCheckpoint writes the final checkpoint.json and subagent transcript.
   181→func (s *GitStore) writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   182→	checkpoint := taskCheckpointData{
   183→		SessionID:      opts.SessionID,
   184→		ToolUseID:      opts.ToolUseID,
   185→		CheckpointUUID: opts.CheckpointUUID,
   186→		AgentID:        opts.AgentID,
   187→	}
   188→	checkpointData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   189→	if err != nil {
   190→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal task checkpoint: %w", err)
   191→	}
   192→	blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, checkpointData)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create task checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	checkpointFile := taskPath + "checkpoint.json"
   198→	entries[checkpointFile] = object.TreeEntry{
   199→		Name: checkpointFile,
   200→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   201→		Hash: blobHash,
   202→	}
   203→
   204→	// Write subagent transcript if available
   205→	if opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != "" && opts.AgentID != "" {
   206→		agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath)
   207→		if readErr == nil {
   208→			// Try JSONL-aware redaction first; fall back to plain string redaction
   209→			// if the content is not valid JSONL (avoids silently dropping the transcript).
   210→			redacted, jsonlErr := redact.JSONLBytes(agentContent)
   211→			if jsonlErr != nil {
   212→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "subagent transcript is not valid JSONL, falling back to plain redaction",
   213→					slog.String("path", opts.SubagentTranscriptPath),
   214→					slog.String("error", jsonlErr.Error()),
   215→				)
   216→				redacted = redact.Bytes(agentContent)
   217→			}
   218→			agentContent = redacted
   219→
   220→			agentBlobHash, agentBlobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent)
   221→			if agentBlobErr == nil {
   222→				agentPath := taskPath + "agent-" + opts.AgentID + ".jsonl"
   223→				entries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   224→					Name: agentPath,
   225→					Mode: filemode.Regular,
   226→					Hash: agentBlobHash,
   227→				}
   228→			}
   229→		}
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	// Return task path without trailing slash
   233→	return taskPath[:len(taskPath)-1], nil
   234→}
   235→
   236→// writeStandardCheckpointEntries writes session files to numbered subdirectories and
   237→// maintains a CheckpointSummary at the root level with aggregated statistics.
   238→//
   239→// Structure:
   240→//
   241→//	basePath/
   242→//	├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   243→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   244→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)
   245→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   246→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   247→//	│   ├── context.md
   248→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   249→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   250→//	└── ...
   251→func (s *GitStore) writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   252→	// Read existing summary to get current session count
   253→	var existingSummary *CheckpointSummary
   254→	metadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   255→	if entry, exists := entries[metadataPath]; exists {
   256→		existing, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   257→		if err == nil {
   258→			existingSummary = existing
   259→		}
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Determine session index: reuse existing slot if session ID matches, otherwise append
   263→	sessionIndex := s.findSessionIndex(basePath, existingSummary, entries, opts.SessionID)
   264→
   265→	// Write session files to numbered subdirectory
   266→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
   267→	sessionFilePaths, err := s.writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts, sessionPath, entries)
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return err
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	// Copy additional metadata files from directory if specified (to session subdirectory)
   273→	if opts.MetadataDir != "" {
   274→		if err := s.copyMetadataDir(opts.MetadataDir, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   275→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy metadata directory: %w", err)
   276→		}
   277→	}
   278→
   279→	// Build the sessions array
   280→	var sessions []SessionFilePaths
   281→	if existingSummary != nil {
   282→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, max(len(existingSummary.Sessions), sessionIndex+1))
   283→		copy(sessions, existingSummary.Sessions)
   284→	} else {
   285→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, 1)
   286→	}
   287→	sessions[sessionIndex] = sessionFilePaths
   288→
   289→	// Update root metadata.json with CheckpointSummary
   290→	return s.writeCheckpointSummary(opts, basePath, entries, sessions)
   291→}
   292→
   293→// writeSessionToSubdirectory writes a single session's files to a numbered subdirectory.
   294→// Returns the absolute file paths from the git tree root for the sessions map.
   295→func (s *GitStore) writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts WriteCommittedOptions, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (SessionFilePaths, error) {
   296→	filePaths := SessionFilePaths{}
   297→
   298→	// Clear any existing entries at this path so stale files from a previous
   299→	// write (e.g. prompt.txt, context.md) don't persist on overwrite.
   300→	for key := range entries {
   301→		if strings.HasPrefix(key, sessionPath) {
   302→			delete(entries, key)
   303→		}
   304→	}
   305→
   306→	// Write transcript
   307→	if err := s.writeTranscript(opts, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   308→		return filePaths, err
   309→	}
   310→	filePaths.Transcript = "/" + sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
   311→	filePaths.ContentHash = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
   312→
   313→	// Write prompts
   314→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
   315→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
   316→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
   317→		if err != nil {
   318→			return filePaths, err
   319→		}
   320→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   321→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
   322→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   323→			Hash: blobHash,
   324→		}
   325→		filePaths.Prompt = "/" + sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Write context
   329→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
   330→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
   331→		if err != nil {
   332→			return filePaths, err
   333→		}
   334→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   335→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
   336→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   337→			Hash: blobHash,
   338→		}
   339→		filePaths.Context = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName
   340→	}
   341→
   342→	// Write session-level metadata.json (CommittedMetadata with all fields including initial_attribution)
   343→	sessionMetadata := CommittedMetadata{
   344→		CheckpointID:                opts.CheckpointID,
   345→		SessionID:                   opts.SessionID,
   346→		Strategy:                    opts.Strategy,
   347→		CreatedAt:                   time.Now().UTC(),
   348→		Branch:                      opts.Branch,
   349→		CheckpointsCount:            opts.CheckpointsCount,
   350→		FilesTouched:                opts.FilesTouched,
   351→		Agent:                       opts.Agent,
   352→		TurnID:                      opts.TurnID,
   353→		IsTask:                      opts.IsTask,
   354→		ToolUseID:                   opts.ToolUseID,
   355→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: opts.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   356→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   357→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:      opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart, // Deprecated: kept for backward compat
   358→		TokenUsage:                  opts.TokenUsage,
   359→		InitialAttribution:          opts.InitialAttribution,
   360→		Summary:                     redactSummary(opts.Summary),
   361→		CLIVersion:                  buildinfo.Version,
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(sessionMetadata, "", "  ")
   365→	if err != nil {
   366→		return filePaths, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session metadata: %w", err)
   367→	}
   368→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   369→	if err != nil {
   370→		return filePaths, err
   371→	}
   372→	entries[sessionPath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   373→		Name: sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   374→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   375→		Hash: metadataHash,
   376→	}
   377→	filePaths.Metadata = "/" + sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName
   378→
   379→	// Write export data (optional — for agents with non-file storage, e.g., OpenCode)
   380→	if len(opts.ExportData) > 0 {
   381→		exportHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, opts.ExportData)
   382→		if err != nil {
   383→			return filePaths, fmt.Errorf("failed to create export data blob: %w", err)
   384→		}
   385→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ExportDataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   386→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ExportDataFileName,
   387→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   388→			Hash: exportHash,
   389→		}
   390→	}
   391→
   392→	return filePaths, nil
   393→}
   394→
   395→// writeCheckpointSummary writes the root-level CheckpointSummary with aggregated statistics.
   396→// sessions is the complete sessions array (already built by the caller).
   397→func (s *GitStore) writeCheckpointSummary(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessions []SessionFilePaths) error {
   398→	checkpointsCount, filesTouched, tokenUsage, err :=
   399→		s.reaggregateFromEntries(basePath, len(sessions), entries)
   400→	if err != nil {
   401→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to aggregate session stats: %w", err)
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	summary := CheckpointSummary{
   405→		CheckpointID:     opts.CheckpointID,
   406→		CLIVersion:       buildinfo.Version,
   407→		Strategy:         opts.Strategy,
   408→		Branch:           opts.Branch,
   409→		CheckpointsCount: checkpointsCount,
   410→		FilesTouched:     filesTouched,
   411→		Sessions:         sessions,
   412→		TokenUsage:       tokenUsage,
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(summary, "", "  ")
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   418→	}
   419→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   420→	if err != nil {
   421→		return err
   422→	}
   423→	entries[basePath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   424→		Name: basePath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   425→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   426→		Hash: metadataHash,
   427→	}
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// findSessionIndex returns the index of an existing session with the given ID,
   432→// or the next available index if not found. This prevents duplicate session entries.
   433→func (s *GitStore) findSessionIndex(basePath string, existingSummary *CheckpointSummary, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessionID string) int {
   434→	if existingSummary == nil {
   435→		return 0
   436→	}
   437→	for i := range len(existingSummary.Sessions) {
   438→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   439→		if entry, exists := entries[path]; exists {
   440→			meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   441→			if err != nil {
   442→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read session metadata during dedup check",
   443→					slog.Int("session_index", i),
   444→					slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   445→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   446→				)
   447→				continue
   448→			}
   449→			if meta.SessionID == sessionID {
   450→				return i
   451→			}
   452→		}
   453→	}
   454→	return len(existingSummary.Sessions)
   455→}
   456→
   457→// reaggregateFromEntries reads all session metadata from the entries map and
   458→// reaggregates CheckpointsCount, FilesTouched, and TokenUsage.
   459→func (s *GitStore) reaggregateFromEntries(basePath string, sessionCount int, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (int, []string, *agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   460→	var totalCount int
   461→	var allFiles []string
   462→	var totalTokens *agent.TokenUsage
   463→
   464→	for i := range sessionCount {
   465→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   466→		entry, exists := entries[path]
   467→		if !exists {
   468→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d metadata not found at %s", i, path)
   469→		}
   470→		meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   471→		if err != nil {
   472→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session %d metadata: %w", i, err)
   473→		}
   474→		totalCount += meta.CheckpointsCount
   475→		allFiles = mergeFilesTouched(allFiles, meta.FilesTouched)
   476→		totalTokens = aggregateTokenUsage(totalTokens, meta.TokenUsage)
   477→	}
   478→
   479→	return totalCount, allFiles, totalTokens, nil
   480→}
   481→
   482→// readJSONFromBlob reads JSON from a blob hash and decodes it to the given type.
   483→func readJSONFromBlob[T any](repo *git.Repository, hash plumbing.Hash) (*T, error) {
   484→	blob, err := repo.BlobObject(hash)
   485→	if err != nil {
   486→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob: %w", err)
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	reader, err := blob.Reader()
   490→	if err != nil {
   491→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob reader: %w", err)
   492→	}
   493→	defer reader.Close()
   494→
   495→	var result T
   496→	if err := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   497→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode: %w", err)
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	return &result, nil
   501→}
   502→
   503→// readSummaryFromBlob reads CheckpointSummary from a blob hash.
   504→func (s *GitStore) readSummaryFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   505→	return readJSONFromBlob[CheckpointSummary](s.repo, hash)
   506→}
   507→
   508→// aggregateTokenUsage sums two TokenUsage structs.
   509→// Returns nil if both inputs are nil.
   510→func aggregateTokenUsage(a, b *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   511→	if a == nil && b == nil {
   512→		return nil
   513→	}
   514→	result := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   515→	if a != nil {
   516→		result.InputTokens = a.InputTokens
   517→		result.CacheCreationTokens = a.CacheCreationTokens
   518→		result.CacheReadTokens = a.CacheReadTokens
   519→		result.OutputTokens = a.OutputTokens
   520→		result.APICallCount = a.APICallCount
   521→	}
   522→	if b != nil {
   523→		result.InputTokens += b.InputTokens
   524→		result.CacheCreationTokens += b.CacheCreationTokens
   525→		result.CacheReadTokens += b.CacheReadTokens
   526→		result.OutputTokens += b.OutputTokens
   527→		result.APICallCount += b.APICallCount
   528→	}
   529→	return result
   530→}
   531→
   532→// writeTranscript writes the transcript file from in-memory content or file path.
   533→// If the transcript exceeds MaxChunkSize, it's split into multiple chunk files.
   534→func (s *GitStore) writeTranscript(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   535→	transcript := opts.Transcript
   536→	if len(transcript) == 0 && opts.TranscriptPath != "" {
   537→		var readErr error
   538→		transcript, readErr = os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath)
   539→		if readErr != nil {
   540→			// Non-fatal: transcript may not exist yet
   541→			transcript = nil
   542→		}
   543→	}
   544→	if len(transcript) == 0 {
   545→		return nil
   546→	}
   547→
   548→	// Redact secrets before chunking so content hash reflects redacted content
   549→	transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(transcript)
   550→	if err != nil {
   551→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
   552→	}
   553→
   554→	// Chunk the transcript if it's too large
   555→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent)
   556→	if err != nil {
   557→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Write chunk files
   561→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   562→		chunkPath := basePath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
   563→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
   564→		if err != nil {
   565→			return err
   566→		}
   567→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   568→			Name: chunkPath,
   569→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   570→			Hash: blobHash,
   571→		}
   572→	}
   573→
   574→	// Content hash for deduplication (hash of full transcript)
   575→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
   576→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
   577→	if err != nil {
   578→		return err
   579→	}
   580→	entries[basePath+paths.ContentHashFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   581→		Name: basePath + paths.ContentHashFileName,
   582→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   583→		Hash: hashBlob,
   584→	}
   585→	return nil
   586→}
   587→
   588→// mergeFilesTouched combines two file lists, removing duplicates.
   589→func mergeFilesTouched(existing, additional []string) []string {
   590→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   591→	var result []string
   592→
   593→	for _, f := range existing {
   594→		if !seen[f] {
   595→			seen[f] = true
   596→			result = append(result, f)
   597→		}
   598→	}
   599→	for _, f := range additional {
   600→		if !seen[f] {
   601→			seen[f] = true
   602→			result = append(result, f)
   603→		}
   604→	}
   605→
   606→	sort.Strings(result)
   607→	return result
   608→}
   609→
   610→// redactSummary returns a copy of the summary with text fields redacted.
   611→// Structural fields (Path, Line, EndLine) are preserved.
   612→// NOTE: When adding new text fields to Summary, LearningsSummary, or CodeLearning,
   613→// update this function to include them in redaction.
   614→func redactSummary(s *Summary) *Summary {
   615→	if s == nil {
   616→		return nil
   617→	}
   618→	return &Summary{
   619→		Intent:    redact.String(s.Intent),
   620→		Outcome:   redact.String(s.Outcome),
   621→		Friction:  redactStringSlice(s.Friction),
   622→		OpenItems: redactStringSlice(s.OpenItems),
   623→		Learnings: LearningsSummary{
   624→			Repo:     redactStringSlice(s.Learnings.Repo),
   625→			Workflow: redactStringSlice(s.Learnings.Workflow),
   626→			Code:     redactCodeLearnings(s.Learnings.Code),
   627→		},
   628→	}
   629→}
   630→
   631→// redactStringSlice applies redact.String to each element.
   632→func redactStringSlice(ss []string) []string {
   633→	if ss == nil {
   634→		return nil
   635→	}
   636→	out := make([]string, len(ss))
   637→	for i, s := range ss {
   638→		out[i] = redact.String(s)
   639→	}
   640→	return out
   641→}
   642→
   643→// redactCodeLearnings redacts only the Finding field, preserving Path/Line/EndLine.
   644→func redactCodeLearnings(cls []CodeLearning) []CodeLearning {
   645→	if cls == nil {
   646→		return nil
   647→	}
   648→	out := make([]CodeLearning, len(cls))
   649→	for i, cl := range cls {
   650→		out[i] = CodeLearning{
   651→			Path:    cl.Path,
   652→			Line:    cl.Line,
   653→			EndLine: cl.EndLine,
   654→			Finding: redact.String(cl.Finding),
   655→		}
   656→	}
   657→	return out
   658→}
   659→
   660→// readMetadataFromBlob reads CommittedMetadata from a blob hash.
   661→func (s *GitStore) readMetadataFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CommittedMetadata, error) {
   662→	return readJSONFromBlob[CommittedMetadata](s.repo, hash)
   663→}
   664→
   665→// buildCommitMessage constructs the commit message with proper trailers.
   666→// The commit subject is always "Checkpoint: <id>" for consistency.
   667→// If CommitSubject is provided (e.g., for task checkpoints), it's included in the body.
   668→func (s *GitStore) buildCommitMessage(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskMetadataPath string) string {
   669→	var commitMsg strings.Builder
   670→
   671→	// Subject line is always the checkpoint ID for consistent formatting
   672→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Checkpoint: %s\n\n", opts.CheckpointID))
   673→
   674→	// Include custom description in body if provided (e.g., task checkpoint details)
   675→	if opts.CommitSubject != "" {
   676→		commitMsg.WriteString(opts.CommitSubject + "\n\n")
   677→	}
   678→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.SessionTrailerKey, opts.SessionID))
   679→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.StrategyTrailerKey, opts.Strategy))
   680→	if opts.Agent != "" {
   681→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.AgentTrailerKey, opts.Agent))
   682→	}
   683→	if opts.EphemeralBranch != "" {
   684→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.EphemeralBranchTrailerKey, opts.EphemeralBranch))
   685→	}
   686→	if taskMetadataPath != "" {
   687→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataPath))
   688→	}
   689→
   690→	return commitMsg.String()
   691→}
   692→
   693→// incrementalCheckpointData represents an incremental checkpoint during subagent execution.
   694→// This mirrors strategy.SubagentCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   695→type incrementalCheckpointData struct {
   696→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
   697→	ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   698→	Timestamp time.Time       `json:"timestamp"`
   699→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
   700→}
   701→
   702→// taskCheckpointData represents a final task checkpoint.
   703→// This mirrors strategy.TaskCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   704→type taskCheckpointData struct {
   705→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   706→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   707→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
   708→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
   709→}
   710→
   711→// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   712→// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
   713→// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
   714→// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   715→//
   716→// The storage format uses numbered subdirectories for each session (0-based):
   717→//
   718→//	<checkpoint-id>/
   719→//	├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary with sessions map
   720→//	├── 0/                 # First session
   721→//	│   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata
   722→//	│   └── full.jsonl     # Transcript
   723→//	├── 1/                 # Second session
   724→//	└── ...
   725→func (s *GitStore) ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   726→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   727→
   728→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   729→	if err != nil {
   730→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // No sessions branch means no checkpoint exists
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   734→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   735→	if err != nil {
   736→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // Checkpoint directory not found
   737→	}
   738→
   739→	// Read root metadata.json as CheckpointSummary
   740→	metadataFile, err := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName)
   741→	if err != nil {
   742→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // metadata.json not found
   743→	}
   744→
   745→	content, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   746→	if err != nil {
   747→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata.json: %w", err)
   748→	}
   749→
   750→	var summary CheckpointSummary
   751→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err != nil {
   752→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata.json: %w", err)
   753→	}
   754→
   755→	return &summary, nil
   756→}
   757→
   758→// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
   759→// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
   760→// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
   761→// Returns an error if the checkpoint or session doesn't exist.
   762→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error) {
   763→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   764→
   765→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   766→	if err != nil {
   767→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   768→	}
   769→
   770→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   771→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   772→	if err != nil {
   773→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   774→	}
   775→
   776→	// Get the session subdirectory
   777→	sessionDir := strconv.Itoa(sessionIndex)
   778→	sessionTree, err := checkpointTree.Tree(sessionDir)
   779→	if err != nil {
   780→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d not found: %w", sessionIndex, err)
   781→	}
   782→
   783→	result := &SessionContent{}
   784→
   785→	// Read session-specific metadata
   786→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   787→	if metadataFile, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   788→		if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   789→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &result.Metadata); jsonErr == nil {
   790→				agentType = result.Metadata.Agent
   791→			}
   792→		}
   793→	}
   794→
   795→	// Read transcript
   796→	if transcript, transcriptErr := readTranscriptFromTree(sessionTree, agentType); transcriptErr == nil && transcript != nil {
   797→		result.Transcript = transcript
   798→	}
   799→
   800→	// Read prompts
   801→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.PromptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   802→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   803→			result.Prompts = content
   804→		}
   805→	}
   806→
   807→	// Read context
   808→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.ContextFileName); fileErr == nil {
   809→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   810→			result.Context = content
   811→		}
   812→	}
   813→
   814→	// Read export data (optional — only present for agents with non-file storage, e.g., OpenCode)
   815→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.ExportDataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   816→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   817→			result.ExportData = []byte(content)
   818→		}
   819→	}
   820→
   821→	return result, nil
   822→}
   823→
   824→// ReadLatestSessionContent is a convenience method that reads the latest session's content.
   825→// This is equivalent to ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, len(summary.Sessions)-1).
   826→func (s *GitStore) ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*SessionContent, error) {
   827→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   828→	if err != nil {
   829→		return nil, err
   830→	}
   831→	if summary == nil {
   832→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   833→	}
   834→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
   835→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint has no sessions: %s", checkpointID)
   836→	}
   837→
   838→	latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   839→	return s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, latestIndex)
   840→}
   841→
   842→// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
   843→// This is useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   844→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   845→// Returns an error if no session with the given ID exists in the checkpoint.
   846→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error) {
   847→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   848→	if err != nil {
   849→		return nil, err
   850→	}
   851→	if summary == nil {
   852→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   853→	}
   854→
   855→	// Iterate through sessions to find the one with matching session ID
   856→	for i := range len(summary.Sessions) {
   857→		content, readErr := s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   858→		if readErr != nil {
   859→			continue
   860→		}
   861→		if content != nil && content.Metadata.SessionID == sessionID {
   862→			return content, nil
   863→		}
   864→	}
   865→
   866→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %q not found in checkpoint %s", sessionID, checkpointID)
   867→}
   868→
   869→// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   870→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
   871→//
   872→
   873→func (s *GitStore) ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error) {
   874→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   875→
   876→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   877→	if err != nil {
   878→		return []CommittedInfo{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch means empty list
   879→	}
   880→
   881→	var checkpoints []CommittedInfo
   882→
   883→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   884→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   885→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   886→			continue
   887→		}
   888→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   889→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   890→			continue
   891→		}
   892→
   893→		bucketTree, treeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   894→		if treeErr != nil {
   895→			continue
   896→		}
   897→
   898→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   899→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   900→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   901→				continue
   902→			}
   903→
   904→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   905→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   906→				continue
   907→			}
   908→
   909→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   910→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   911→			checkpointID, cpIDErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   912→			if cpIDErr != nil {
   913→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs (shouldn't happen with our own data)
   914→				continue
   915→			}
   916→
   917→			info := CommittedInfo{
   918→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   919→			}
   920→
   921→			// Get details from root metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   922→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   923→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   924→					var summary CheckpointSummary
   925→					if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   926→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   927→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   928→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   929→
   930→						// Read session metadata from latest session to get Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   931→						if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   932→							latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   933→							latestDir := strconv.Itoa(latestIndex)
   934→							if sessionTree, treeErr := checkpointTree.Tree(latestDir); treeErr == nil {
   935→								if sessionMetadataFile, smErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); smErr == nil {
   936→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionMetadataFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   937→										var sessionMetadata CommittedMetadata
   938→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   939→											info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   940→											info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   941→											info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   942→										}
   943→									}
   944→								}
   945→							}
   946→						}
   947→					}
   948→				}
   949→			}
   950→
   951→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   952→		}
   953→	}
   954→
   955→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   956→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   957→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   958→	})
   959→
   960→	return checkpoints, nil
   961→}
   962→
   963→// GetTranscript retrieves the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
   964→// Returns the latest session's transcript.
   965→func (s *GitStore) GetTranscript(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
   966→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID)
   967→	if err != nil {
   968→		return nil, err
   969→	}
   970→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   971→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   972→	}
   973→	return content.Transcript, nil
   974→}
   975→
   976→// GetSessionLog retrieves the session transcript, session ID, and export data for a checkpoint.
   977→// This is the primary method for looking up session logs by checkpoint ID.
   978→// The export data is optional — only present for agents with non-file storage (e.g., OpenCode).
   979→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   980→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   981→func (s *GitStore) GetSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, []byte, error) {
   982→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID)
   983→	if err != nil {
   984→		if errors.Is(err, ErrCheckpointNotFound) {
   985→			return nil, "", nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   986→		}
   987→		return nil, "", nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   988→	}
   989→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   990→		return nil, "", nil, ErrNoTranscript
   991→	}
   992→	return content.Transcript, content.Metadata.SessionID, content.ExportData, nil
   993→}
   994→
   995→// LookupSessionLog is a convenience function that opens the repository and retrieves
   996→// a session log by checkpoint ID. This is the primary entry point for callers that
   997→// don't already have a GitStore instance.
   998→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   999→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
  1000→func LookupSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, []byte, error) {
  1001→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
  1002→	if err != nil {
  1003→		return nil, "", nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
  1004→	}
  1005→	store := NewGitStore(repo)
  1006→	return store.GetSessionLog(cpID)
  1007→}
  1008→
  1009→// UpdateSummary updates the summary field in the latest session's metadata.
  1010→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
  1011→func (s *GitStore) UpdateSummary(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *Summary) error {
  1012→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
  1013→
  1014→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
  1015→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
  1016→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
  1017→	}
  1018→
  1019→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
  1020→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
  1021→	if err != nil {
  1022→		return err
  1023→	}
  1024→
  1025→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the latest session
  1026→	basePath := checkpointID.Path() + "/"
  1027→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
  1028→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
  1029→	if !exists {
  1030→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1031→	}
  1032→
  1033→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
  1034→	if err != nil {
  1035→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
  1036→	}
  1037→
  1038→	// Find the latest session's metadata path (0-based indexing)
  1039→	latestIndex := len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
  1040→	sessionMetadataPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, latestIndex, paths.MetadataFileName)
  1041→	sessionEntry, exists := entries[sessionMetadataPath]
  1042→	if !exists {
  1043→		return fmt.Errorf("session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
  1044→	}
  1045→
  1046→	// Read and update session metadata
  1047→	existingMetadata, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(sessionEntry.Hash)
  1048→	if err != nil {
  1049→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read session metadata: %w", err)
  1050→	}
  1051→
  1052→	// Update the summary
  1053→	existingMetadata.Summary = redactSummary(summary)
  1054→
  1055→	// Write updated session metadata
  1056→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(existingMetadata, "", "  ")
  1057→	if err != nil {
  1058→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal metadata: %w", err)
  1059→	}
  1060→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
  1061→	if err != nil {
  1062→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata blob: %w", err)
  1063→	}
  1064→	entries[sessionMetadataPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1065→		Name: sessionMetadataPath,
  1066→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1067→		Hash: metadataHash,
  1068→	}
  1069→
  1070→	// Build and commit
  1071→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
  1072→	if err != nil {
  1073→		return err
  1074→	}
  1075→
  1076→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1077→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Update summary for checkpoint %s (session: %s)", checkpointID, existingMetadata.SessionID)
  1078→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
  1079→	if err != nil {
  1080→		return err
  1081→	}
  1082→
  1083→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1084→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1085→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1086→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1087→	}
  1088→
  1089→	return nil
  1090→}
  1091→
  1092→// UpdateCommitted replaces the transcript, prompts, and context for an existing
  1093→// committed checkpoint. Uses replace semantics: the full session transcript is
  1094→// written, replacing whatever was stored at initial condensation time.
  1095→//
  1096→// This is called at stop time to finalize all checkpoints from the current turn
  1097→// with the complete session transcript (from prompt to stop event).
  1098→//
  1099→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
  1100→func (s *GitStore) UpdateCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts UpdateCommittedOptions) error {
  1101→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
  1102→		return errors.New("invalid update options: checkpoint ID is required")
  1103→	}
  1104→
  1105→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
  1106→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
  1107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
  1108→	}
  1109→
  1110→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
  1111→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
  1112→	if err != nil {
  1113→		return err
  1114→	}
  1115→
  1116→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the session slot
  1117→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
  1118→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
  1119→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
  1120→	if !exists {
  1121→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1122→	}
  1123→
  1124→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
  1125→	if err != nil {
  1126→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
  1127→	}
  1128→	if len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) == 0 {
  1129→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
  1130→	}
  1131→
  1132→	// Find session index matching opts.SessionID
  1133→	sessionIndex := -1
  1134→	for i := range len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) {
  1135→		metaPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
  1136→		if metaEntry, metaExists := entries[metaPath]; metaExists {
  1137→			meta, metaErr := s.readMetadataFromBlob(metaEntry.Hash)
  1138→			if metaErr == nil && meta.SessionID == opts.SessionID {
  1139→				sessionIndex = i
  1140→				break
  1141→			}
  1142→		}
  1143→	}
  1144→	if sessionIndex == -1 {
  1145→		// Fall back to latest session; log so mismatches are diagnosable.
  1146→		sessionIndex = len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
  1147→		logging.Debug(ctx, "UpdateCommitted: session ID not found, falling back to latest",
  1148→			slog.String("session_id", opts.SessionID),
  1149→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", string(opts.CheckpointID)),
  1150→			slog.Int("fallback_index", sessionIndex),
  1151→		)
  1152→	}
  1153→
  1154→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
  1155→
  1156→	// Replace transcript (full replace, not append)
  1157→	// Apply redaction as safety net (caller should redact, but we ensure it here)
  1158→	if len(opts.Transcript) > 0 {
  1159→		transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.Transcript)
  1160→		if err != nil {
  1161→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
  1162→		}
  1163→		if err := s.replaceTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
  1164→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to replace transcript: %w", err)
  1165→		}
  1166→	}
  1167→
  1168→	// Replace prompts (apply redaction as safety net)
  1169→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
  1170→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
  1171→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
  1172→		if err != nil {
  1173→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create prompt blob: %w", err)
  1174→		}
  1175→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
  1176→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
  1177→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1178→			Hash: blobHash,
  1179→		}
  1180→	}
  1181→
  1182→	// Replace context (apply redaction as safety net)
  1183→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
  1184→		contextBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
  1185→		if err != nil {
  1186→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context blob: %w", err)
  1187→		}
  1188→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
  1189→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
  1190→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1191→			Hash: contextBlob,
  1192→		}
  1193→	}
  1194→
  1195→	// Build and commit
  1196→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
  1197→	if err != nil {
  1198→		return err
  1199→	}
  1200→
  1201→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1202→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Finalize transcript for Checkpoint: %s", opts.CheckpointID)
  1203→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
  1204→	if err != nil {
  1205→		return err
  1206→	}
  1207→
  1208→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1209→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1210→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1211→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1212→	}
  1213→
  1214→	return nil
  1215→}
  1216→
  1217→// replaceTranscript writes the full transcript content, replacing any existing transcript.
  1218→// Also removes any chunk files from a previous write and updates the content hash.
  1219→func (s *GitStore) replaceTranscript(transcript []byte, agentType agent.AgentType, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1220→	// Remove existing transcript files (base + any chunks)
  1221→	transcriptBase := sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
  1222→	for key := range entries {
  1223→		if key == transcriptBase || strings.HasPrefix(key, transcriptBase+".") {
  1224→			delete(entries, key)
  1225→		}
  1226→	}
  1227→
  1228→	// Chunk the transcript (matches writeTranscript behavior)
  1229→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, agentType)
  1230→	if err != nil {
  1231→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
  1232→	}
  1233→
  1234→	// Write chunk files
  1235→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
  1236→		chunkPath := sessionPath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
  1237→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
  1238→		if err != nil {
  1239→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create transcript blob: %w", err)
  1240→		}
  1241→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1242→			Name: chunkPath,
  1243→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1244→			Hash: blobHash,
  1245→		}
  1246→	}
  1247→
  1248→	// Update content hash
  1249→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
  1250→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
  1251→	if err != nil {
  1252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create content hash blob: %w", err)
  1253→	}
  1254→	hashPath := sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
  1255→	entries[hashPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1256→		Name: hashPath,
  1257→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
  1258→		Hash: hashBlob,
  1259→	}
  1260→
  1261→	return nil
  1262→}
  1263→
  1264→// ensureSessionsBranch ensures the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists.
  1265→func (s *GitStore) ensureSessionsBranch() error {
  1266→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1267→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1268→	if err == nil {
  1269→		return nil // Branch exists
  1270→	}
  1271→
  1272→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
  1273→	emptyTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
  1274→	if err != nil {
  1275→		return err
  1276→	}
  1277→
  1278→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1279→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize sessions branch", authorName, authorEmail)
  1280→	if err != nil {
  1281→		return err
  1282→	}
  1283→
  1284→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
  1285→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1286→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1287→	}
  1288→	return nil
  1289→}
  1290→
  1291→// getSessionsBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1292→// Falls back to origin/entire/checkpoints/v1 if the local branch doesn't exist.
  1293→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
  1294→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1295→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1296→	if err != nil {
  1297→		// Local branch doesn't exist, try remote-tracking branch
  1298→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1299→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
  1300→		if err != nil {
  1301→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
  1302→		}
  1303→	}
  1304→
  1305→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1306→	if err != nil {
  1307→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
  1308→	}
  1309→
  1310→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1311→	if err != nil {
  1312→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
  1313→	}
  1314→
  1315→	return tree, nil
  1316→}
  1317→
  1318→// CreateBlobFromContent creates a blob object from in-memory content.
  1319→// Exported for use by strategy package (session_test.go)
  1320→func CreateBlobFromContent(repo *git.Repository, content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1321→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1322→	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
  1323→	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))
  1324→
  1325→	writer, err := obj.Writer()
  1326→	if err != nil {
  1327→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get object writer: %w", err)
  1328→	}
  1329→
  1330→	_, err = writer.Write(content)
  1331→	if err != nil {
  1332→		_ = writer.Close()
  1333→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write blob content: %w", err)
  1334→	}
  1335→	if err := writer.Close(); err != nil {
  1336→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to close blob writer: %w", err)
  1337→	}
  1338→
  1339→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1340→	if err != nil {
  1341→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store blob object: %w", err)
  1342→	}
  1343→	return hash, nil
  1344→}
  1345→
  1346→// copyMetadataDir copies all files from a directory to the checkpoint path.
  1347→// Used to include additional metadata files like task checkpoints, subagent transcripts, etc.
  1348→func (s *GitStore) copyMetadataDir(metadataDir, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1349→	err := filepath.Walk(metadataDir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
  1350→		if err != nil {
  1351→			return err
  1352→		}
  1353→
  1354→		// Skip symlinks to prevent reading files outside the metadata directory.
  1355→		// A symlink could point to sensitive files (e.g., /etc/passwd) which would
  1356→		// then be captured in the checkpoint and stored in git history.
  1357→		// NOTE: filepath.Walk uses os.Stat (follows symlinks), so info.Mode() never
  1358→		// reports ModeSymlink. We use os.Lstat to check the entry itself.
  1359→		// This check MUST come before IsDir() because Walk follows symlinked
  1360→		// directories and would recurse into them otherwise.
  1361→		linfo, lstatErr := os.Lstat(path)
  1362→		if lstatErr != nil {
  1363→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to lstat %s: %w", path, lstatErr)
  1364→		}
  1365→		if linfo.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
  1366→			if info.IsDir() {
  1367→				return filepath.SkipDir
  1368→			}
  1369→			return nil
  1370→		}
  1371→
  1372→		if info.IsDir() {
  1373→			return nil
  1374→		}
  1375→
  1376→		// Get relative path within metadata dir
  1377→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(metadataDir, path)
  1378→		if err != nil {
  1379→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get relative path for %s: %w", path, err)
  1380→		}
  1381→
  1382→		// Prevent path traversal via symlinks pointing outside the metadata dir
  1383→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
  1384→			return fmt.Errorf("path traversal detected: %s", relPath)
  1385→		}
  1386→
  1387→		// Create blob from file with secrets redaction
  1388→		blobHash, mode, err := createRedactedBlobFromFile(s.repo, path, relPath)
  1389→		if err != nil {
  1390→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob for %s: %w", path, err)
  1391→		}
  1392→
  1393→		// Store at checkpoint path (use forward slashes for git tree compatibility on Windows)
  1394→		fullPath := basePath + filepath.ToSlash(relPath)
  1395→		entries[fullPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1396→			Name: fullPath,
  1397→			Mode: mode,
  1398→			Hash: blobHash,
  1399→		}
  1400→
  1401→		return nil
  1402→	})
  1403→	if err != nil {
  1404→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to walk metadata directory: %w", err)
  1405→	}
  1406→	return nil
  1407→}
  1408→
  1409→// createRedactedBlobFromFile reads a file, applies secrets redaction, and creates a git blob.
  1410→// JSONL files get JSONL-aware redaction; all other files get plain string redaction.
  1411→func createRedactedBlobFromFile(repo *git.Repository, filePath, treePath string) (plumbing.Hash, filemode.FileMode, error) {
  1412→	info, err := os.Stat(filePath)
  1413→	if err != nil {
  1414→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to stat file: %w", err)
  1415→	}
  1416→
  1417→	mode := filemode.Regular
  1418→	if info.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
  1419→		mode = filemode.Executable
  1420→	}
  1421→
  1422→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // filePath comes from walking the metadata directory
  1423→	if err != nil {
  1424→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read file: %w", err)
  1425→	}
  1426→
  1427→	// Skip redaction for binary files — they can't contain text secrets and
  1428→	// running string replacement on them would corrupt the data.
  1429→	isBin, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(content))
  1430→	if binErr != nil || isBin {
  1431→		hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1432→		if err != nil {
  1433→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1434→		}
  1435→		return hash, mode, nil
  1436→	}
  1437→
  1438→	if strings.HasSuffix(treePath, ".jsonl") {
  1439→		redacted, jsonlErr := redact.JSONLBytes(content)
  1440→		if jsonlErr != nil {
  1441→			redacted = redact.Bytes(content)
  1442→		}
  1443→		content = redacted
  1444→	} else {
  1445→		content = redact.Bytes(content)
  1446→	}
  1447→
  1448→	hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1449→	if err != nil {
  1450→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1451→	}
  1452→	return hash, mode, nil
  1453→}
  1454→
  1455→// GetGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email,
  1456→// checking both the repository-local config and the global ~/.gitconfig.
  1457→func GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1458→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1459→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1460→	if err == nil {
  1461→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1462→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1463→	}
  1464→
  1465→	// If not found in local config, try global config
  1466→	if name == "" || email == "" {
  1467→		globalCfg, err := config.LoadConfig(config.GlobalScope)
  1468→		if err == nil {
  1469→			if name == "" {
  1470→				name = globalCfg.User.Name
  1471→			}
  1472→			if email == "" {
  1473→				email = globalCfg.User.Email
  1474→			}
  1475→		}
  1476→	}
  1477→
  1478→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1479→	if name == "" {
  1480→		name = "Unknown"
  1481→	}
  1482→	if email == "" {
  1483→		email = "unknown@local"
  1484→	}
  1485→
  1486→	return name, email
  1487→}
  1488→
  1489→// readTranscriptFromTree reads a transcript from a git tree, handling both chunked and non-chunked formats.
  1490→// It checks for chunk files first (.001, .002, etc.), then falls back to the base file.
  1491→// The agentType is used for reassembling chunks in the correct format.
  1492→func readTranscriptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, agentType agent.AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
  1493→	// Collect all transcript-related files
  1494→	var chunkFiles []string
  1495→	var hasBaseFile bool
  1496→
  1497→	for _, entry := range tree.Entries {
  1498→		if entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileName || entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy {
  1499→			hasBaseFile = true
  1500→		}
  1501→		// Check for chunk files (full.jsonl.001, full.jsonl.002, etc.)
  1502→		if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName+".") {
  1503→			idx := agent.ParseChunkIndex(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1504→			if idx > 0 {
  1505→				chunkFiles = append(chunkFiles, entry.Name)
  1506→			}
  1507→		}
  1508→	}
  1509→
  1510→	// If we have chunk files, read and reassemble them
  1511→	if len(chunkFiles) > 0 {
  1512→		// Sort chunk files by index
  1513→		chunkFiles = agent.SortChunkFiles(chunkFiles, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1514→
  1515→		// Check if base file should be included as chunk 0.
  1516→		// NOTE: This assumes the chunking convention where the unsuffixed file
  1517→		// (full.jsonl) is chunk 0, and numbered files (.001, .002) are chunks 1+.
  1518→		if hasBaseFile {
  1519→			chunkFiles = append([]string{paths.TranscriptFileName}, chunkFiles...)
  1520→		}
  1521→
  1522→		var chunks [][]byte
  1523→		for _, chunkFile := range chunkFiles {
  1524→			file, err := tree.File(chunkFile)
  1525→			if err != nil {
  1526→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk file from tree",
  1527→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1528→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1529→				)
  1530→				continue
  1531→			}
  1532→			content, err := file.Contents()
  1533→			if err != nil {
  1534→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk contents",
  1535→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1536→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1537→				)
  1538→				continue
  1539→			}
  1540→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(content))
  1541→		}
  1542→
  1543→		if len(chunks) > 0 {
  1544→			result, err := agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)
  1545→			if err != nil {
  1546→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to reassemble transcript: %w", err)
  1547→			}
  1548→			return result, nil
  1549→		}
  1550→	}
  1551→
  1552→	// Fall back to reading base file (non-chunked or backwards compatibility)
  1553→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileName); err == nil {
  1554→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1555→			return []byte(content), nil
  1556→		}
  1557→	}
  1558→
  1559→	// Try legacy filename
  1560→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); err == nil {
  1561→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1562→			return []byte(content), nil
  1563→		}
  1564→	}
  1565→
  1566→	return nil, nil
  1567→}
  1568→
  1569→// Author contains author information for a checkpoint.
  1570→type Author struct {
  1571→	Name  string
  1572→	Email string
  1573→}
  1574→
  1575→// GetCheckpointAuthor retrieves the author of a checkpoint from the entire/checkpoints/v1 commit history.
  1576→// Returns the author of the commit that introduced this checkpoint's metadata.json file.
  1577→// Returns empty Author if the checkpoint is not found or the sessions branch doesn't exist.
  1578→func (s *GitStore) GetCheckpointAuthor(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (Author, error) {
  1579→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
  1580→
  1581→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1582→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1583→	if err != nil {
  1584→		return Author{}, nil
  1585→	}
  1586→
  1587→	// Path to the checkpoint's metadata file
  1588→	metadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
  1589→
  1590→	// Walk commit history looking for the commit that introduced this file
  1591→	iter, err := s.repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
  1592→		From:  ref.Hash(),
  1593→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
  1594→	})
  1595→	if err != nil {
  1596→		return Author{}, nil
  1597→	}
  1598→	defer iter.Close()
  1599→
  1600→	var author Author
  1601→	var foundCommit *object.Commit
  1602→
  1603→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
  1604→		tree, treeErr := c.Tree()
  1605→		if treeErr != nil {
  1606→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip commits we can't read, continue searching
  1607→		}
  1608→
  1609→		_, fileErr := tree.File(metadataPath)
  1610→		if fileErr != nil {
  1611→			// File doesn't exist in this commit - we've gone past the creation point
  1612→			if foundCommit != nil {
  1613→				return errStopIteration
  1614→			}
  1615→			return nil
  1616→		}
  1617→
  1618→		// File exists - track it (oldest one with file is the creator)
  1619→		foundCommit = c
  1620→		author = Author{
  1621→			Name:  c.Author.Name,
  1622→			Email: c.Author.Email,
  1623→		}
  1624→		return nil
  1625→	})
  1626→
  1627→	// Ignore errStopIteration - it's just for early exit
  1628→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
  1629→		return Author{}, nil
  1630→	}
  1631→
  1632→	return author, nil
  1633→}
  1634→
